                                                                    Infrastructure sprint
                                                                        Dangote referenced the Kenyan pipeline deal as part of a

                                                                    wider set of infrastructure investments being advanced by the
                                                                    group, which has targeted $50 billion of new spending as part of
                                                                    its Vision 2030 strategy. Alongside its Kenyan refinery project in
                                                                    Lamu, the company is also working on new pipeline links between
                                                                    Djibouti and Ethiopia, as well as Namibia, Zimbabwe and Zambia.

                                                                        To support its access to African markets and beyond, the
                                                                    company is additionally building tank farms in Djibouti, Cameroon,
                                                                    and Namibia, among others, the group founder said, without
                                                                    providing further timelines on the company's myriad construction
                                                                    projects.

                                                                        The East African expansion comes as part of a push to tap
                                                                    unserved inland markets that are growing fast due to population
                                                                    growth and economic development, Dangote said in London.

                                                                        "When you look at the eastern part of Africa, they need more
                                                                    refining than the western part, because they are into a lot of
                                                                    mining, and it is difficult for them to get diesel. The only thing that
                                                                    isn't a mature market is aviation, for which there's now a lot of
                                                                    market out of Africa," he said.

                                                                        Uganda currently consumes roughly 45,000 b/d of refined
                                                                    products, although the volume could reach 65,000 b/d by 2035,
                                                                    according to estimates from S&P Global Energy CERA analysts.
